Annual Health Checkup Report

Patient: Marcus Ellery (MRN-47892)
Date of Birth: 1979-01-22 (Age: 47)
Checkup Date: 2026-04-15
Report ID: ACH-2026-0415-ME47
Ordering Physician: Dr. Priya Nambiar
Institution: Aldgate Corporate Health Ltd., Merton Branch

Executive Summary

The annual biochemical panel reveals markedly elevated liver transaminases. ALT is 187 U/L (reference: 10–40 U/L; 4.7× ULN) and AST is 124 U/L (reference: 10–40 U/L; 3.1× ULN). All other parameters are within normal limits. The hepatocellular pattern of injury warrants clinical correlation and specialist review. No cholestatic component is evident from the current panel. An ultrasound examination was performed on the same date; see imaging report section below.

Imaging — Abdominal Ultrasound

Technique: B-mode abdominal ultrasound, transabdominal approach.

Findings: The liver parenchyma demonstrates mildly increased echogenicity consistent with mild hepatic steatosis without focal lesion. No biliary duct dilatation. Gallbladder: unremarkable. Spleen: 10.2 cm, normal. No free peritoneal fluid. Kidneys: bilateral, normal in size and echogenicity.

Impression: Mild hepatic steatosis without focal lesion. Clinical correlation with liver function tests recommended. No evidence of hepatic mass or cirrhotic changes on ultrasound.

Appendix: Normal Ranges Reference

All reference ranges are derived from the Aldgate Corporate Health standardised adult male reference set (version 2024-Q4), calibrated against IFCC recommendations for enzymatic assays at 37°C.

ALT upper limit of normal (ULN): 40 U/L. Values exceeding 3× ULN trigger automatic specialist referral advisory. Values exceeding 5× ULN require same-day clinical review.
